- **proguard**: - The UUID is **deterministically derived from the mapping file contents** — identical files always produce the same UUID. This is the same value Sentry uses to associate a mapping with obfuscated Android stack traces. - This matches the UUID computed by the legacy `sentry-cli proguard uuid` command byte-for-byte.
